## Overview & Philosophy
Instead of treating AI video generation as probabilistic text-to-diffusion gambling (with morphing fingers, floating artifacts, and illegible text), **Opus Video Director** approaches video as **deterministic software composition**.

By authoring code (Canvas API, SVG, GSAP, Three.js, HyperFrames, or Remotion) driven by closed-form functions of time $f(t)$ and capturing every frame deterministically at 60fps in headless Chromium, you achieve:
- **Pixel-Perfect Typography & Wordmarks**: SVGs stay needle-sharp at any resolution.
- **Genuine UI Realism**: Recreate actual SaaS cockpits, iOS simulators, and database metrics without hallucinated interfaces.
- **100% Deterministic Reproducibility**: Seek to any millisecond without dropped frames or drift.
- **Infinite Resolution & Aspect Ratios**: Render 16:9 for desktop and 9:16 for TikTok/Reels from the exact same mathematical state.

## Quick Start & CLI Engine
The repository includes `scripts/opus_videos.py`, an autonomous CLI helper to explore and scaffold projects:

```bash
# 1. View global catalog statistics
python3 scripts/opus_videos.py stats

# 2. Search across 475 video recipes by keyword
python3 scripts/opus_videos.py search "taxtello"
python3 scripts/opus_videos.py search "minecraft"
python3 scripts/opus_videos.py search "apple"

# 3. Inspect a specific video's complete metadata & prompt
python3 scripts/opus_videos.py inspect "daniel-haida-636937"

# 4. Print clean, ready-to-run prompt instructions
python3 scripts/opus_videos.py prompt "ik-builds-585923"

# 5. Scaffold a production project boilerplate
python3 scripts/opus_videos.py scaffold saas-launch --target-dir ./my-launch --stack canvas
python3 scripts/opus_videos.py scaffold motion-showreel --target-dir ./my-reel --stack hyperframes
```

## 🎬 Agente Especializado: `video-director-cinematographer`

El proyecto incluye un agente canónico multi-entorno (**Claude Code**, **Antigravity** y **Codex**) diseñado para actuar como un **Director Cinematográfico y Diseñador Visual Senior**.

### ¿Qué hace este agente?
1. **Acomoda la información desordenada**: Toma notas sueltas, briefs caóticos o ideas vagas y las estructura en un guion técnico segundo por segundo (0-3s Hook, 3-8s Demostración, 8-12s Hero Shot, 12-15s Outro).
2. **Selecciona el estilo óptimo**: Conoce a fondo los 475 estilos de Opus 5.5 y consulta la CLI (`opus_videos.py`) para sugerir el arquetipo y video de referencia exacto para tu producto o tema.
3. **Audita insumos faltantes antes de ejecutar**: Antes de redactar prompts o generar código, revisa la lista de insumos técnicos necesarios y te pregunta exactamente qué logos (SVG), paletas (HEX), capturas de pantalla o audios necesitas cargar para que el prompt funcione sin alucinaciones.
4. **Redacta prompts de 7 capas**: Produce prompts cinematográficos con distancias focales reales, iluminación física, curvas de aceleración cubic-bezier y locuciones calibradas matemáticamente a 135 WPM (con regla de cero emojis).

### ¿Cómo convocarlo?
- **En Antigravity**: El subagente `video-director-cinematographer` está disponible directamente para ser invocado o consultado en cualquier sesión.
- **En Claude Code**: `~/.claude/agents/video-director-cinematographer.md`
- **En Codex**: `~/.codex/agents/video-director-cinematographer.toml`

## Core Architectural Contract

### 1. Closed-Form Spring Dynamics (Seek-Safe)
Never use `setTimeout` or mutable frame loops. All animations must be computed from time:
```javascript
function spring(t, delay = 0, omega = 10.0) {
  if (t < delay) return 0;
  const tau = t - delay;
  return 1 - (1 + omega * tau) * Math.exp(-omega * tau);
}

window.seek = function(t) {
  // Pure function of t. Everything drawn at time t is mathematically pure.
};
```

### 2. Object-Driven Transitions
Reject generic transitions like crossfades and slide-lefts. Every transition must be an object morph:
- Vertex in chart line $\to$ corner of financial invoice
- Wordmark collapses into period $\to$ period expands into circular viewport
- Notch expands into browser window $\to$ tab shrinks into mobile card

### 3. Restrained Palette & Luxury Contrast
- **Background**: Deep machined metal (`#0c0f1a`, `#111525`).
- **Precious Accent**: Brand Gold (`#e8b84b`) or Electric Cyan (`#00e5ff`). Accent is used for active state only.
- **Typography**: Primary Warm White (`#f6f4eb`), Secondary Muted (`#8b93b5`).

## 14 Video Archetypes

### <a id="saas-launch"></a> SaaS & Product Launch Film (`saas-launch`) - 30 Videos
**Description**: High-end product reveals with dark metal/gold aesthetics, dimensional UI cards, magnetic snapping, macro camera moves, and zero template feel.
**Keywords**: `saas, launch, product, taxtello, redship, feature, cockpit`

### <a id="motion-showreel"></a> Kinetic Motion Designer Showreel (`motion-showreel`) - 218 Videos
**Description**: 15-60s adrenaline showreels showcasing typography, UI slices, transitions, fast cuts, and peak motion design capabilities.
**Keywords**: `showreel, motion, resume, designer, reel, dynamic, go all out`

### <a id="kinetic-typography"></a> Architectural Kinetic Typography & Spoken Word (`kinetic-typography`) - 8 Videos
**Description**: Words as physical structural objects (lintel, platform, support, aperture) reacting to voice rhythm with letterform deformation.
**Keywords**: `typography, type, kinetic, words, speech, text, poem`

### <a id="app-mobile-ui"></a> Mobile App UI Tour & Micro-Interactions (`app-mobile-ui`) - 17 Videos
**Description**: Phone bezels, dynamic islands, tab transitions, realistic cursor clicks, long-presses, and fluid morphs across mobile views.
**Keywords**: `ios, mobile, app, simulator, iphone, screen, notch`

### <a id="concept-explainer"></a> Technical, Business & Scientific Explainer (`concept-explainer`) - 46 Videos
**Description**: Step-by-step visual dissection of complex technical architectures, developer workflows, or scientific systems with animated diagrams.
**Keywords**: `explainer, explain, how it works, guide, tutorial, architecture, system`

### <a id="data-viz-charts"></a> Data Visualization & Financial Heatmaps (`data-viz-charts`) - 2 Videos
**Description**: Live drawing charts, animated KPI counters, candlestick trajectories, financial cockpit sweeps, and glowing data nodes.
**Keywords**: `chart, data, graph, metric, finance, revenue, analytics`

### <a id="map-geo-spatial"></a> Geographic, Satellite & 3D Globe Flythroughs (`map-geo-spatial`) - 5 Videos
**Description**: Zoom from planetary orbit down to city streets, route tracking, glowing location pins, and geospatial flight paths.
**Keywords**: `map, globe, geo, city, satellite, earth, flight`

### <a id="3d-world-flythrough"></a> 3D Environments, Dioramas & Infinite Zooms (`3d-world-flythrough`) - 20 Videos
**Description**: Infinite zoom through portals/objects, 3D architectural walk-throughs, low-poly isometric dioramas, and Blender camera rigs.
**Keywords**: `infinite zoom, flythrough, camera, blender, scene, room, house`

### <a id="shader-generative"></a> GLSL Shaders & Real-time Generative Art (`shader-generative`) - 51 Videos
**Description**: GPU-accelerated raymarching, volumetric fluid/fire simulation, blackbody radiation formulas, domain warping, and audio-reactive fragment shaders.
**Keywords**: `shader, glsl, generative, fluid, fire, smoke, raymarch`

### <a id="particles-physics"></a> Particle Systems, Verlet Physics & Collisions (`particles-physics`) - 14 Videos
**Description**: Closed-form spring dynamics, gravity wells, elastic cloth simulation, particle swarms, and rigid-body ball collisions.
**Keywords**: `particle, particles, physics, gravity, collision, simulation, ball`

### <a id="audio-reactive-mv"></a> Audio-Reactive Music Videos & Glitch MV (`audio-reactive-mv`) - 26 Videos
**Description**: 120 BPM beat-locked cuts, kaleidoscope mirrors, RGB splitting on drops, white flash on downbeats, and frequency spectrum displacement.
**Keywords**: `music, bpm, song, audio, track, beat, reactive`

### <a id="playable-game-arcade"></a> Playable Web Games & Arcade Simulations (`playable-game-arcade`) - 20 Videos
**Description**: Top-down 90s racing games, playable browser Minecraft clones, retro platformers, and automated gameplay cinematic captures.
**Keywords**: `game, playable, arcade, minecraft, racing, car, control`

### <a id="brand-commercial-ad"></a> Cinematic Storytelling & High-Concept Ads (`brand-commercial-ad`) - 14 Videos
**Description**: Emotionally resonant multi-scene cinematic narratives (e.g. astronaut and station porthole meteor strike, luxury timepiece reveals).
**Keywords**: `commercial, cm, ad, brand, apple, luxury, film`

### <a id="character-retro-pixel"></a> Character Mascot Animation & Retro Pixel Worlds (`character-retro-pixel`) - 4 Videos
**Description**: Hand-drawn canvas vector characters, 8-bit/16-bit pixel sprites, sticker outlines, and playful mascot motion loops.
**Keywords**: `character, pixel, mascot, bear, retro, 8-bit, 16-bit`

| <a href="https://x.com/op7418/status/2103724883301814408"><img src="https://media.skillry.dev/opus-5-5/op7418-814408/remake.8eccd918b8.webp" width="200" alt="op7418-814408" loading="lazy" /></a> | <b>ID</b>: #143<br><b>Slug</b>: <code>op7418-814408</code><br><b>Author</b>: <a href="https://x.com/op7418/status/2103724883301814408">@op7418</a><br><b>Duration</b>: 14.74s (16:9)<br><b>Tags</b>: `threejs` `shader` `canvas` `gsap` | <details><summary><b>View Prompt</b></summary><br><small>帮我做一个原创的手游「高光时刻」动效：[主体，例如：开宝箱 / 段位晋升 / 成就解锁 / Boss 掉落 / 角色升级]。<br>做成一个可以直接打开的单文件网页（HTML + CSS + JS），点一下就能完整播放，效果对标商业手游的结算 / 奖励演出。<br><br>【世界观与风格】<br>- 原创美术，不要模仿任何现有游戏的 IP、角色或 Logo。<br>- 卡通手游风：高饱和配色，统一的深色描边（例如 #1A1033），圆润厚重的造型。<br>- 配色随等级升级：[等级阶梯，例如：普通绿 → 稀有蓝 → 史诗紫 → 传说金]。每升一级都要切换主色、光效颜色和背景色调。<br>- 标题用圆胖的游戏字体（如 Titan One / Lilita One），描黑边，加投影；文字逐字「砸」进画面，带回弹。<br><br>【主体物：做成真 3D】<br>- 用 Three.js 建模，不要用平面 SVG 贴图。要有厚度、倒角，以及符合主体的结构细节（木板缝、铆钉、包边、宝石切面、锁扣等）；木板缝这类细节用真实几何体拼出来，不要贴黑线条。<br>- 着色用卡通分阶（MeshToonMaterial 加 3～4 阶的渐变贴图），配三种灯光：主光、天光、等级色的轮廓光。<br>- 描边用后期边缘检测：渲染法线图 + 深度图，再用着色器画线。外轮廓粗、零件交界细，粗细全程一致；另外做 1.5 倍超采样和多重采样抗锯齿。<br>  不要用「放大一圈的黑色背面」那种描边，那样粗细不均、转角会断。<br>  深度判断的阈值要放宽，接缝主要靠法线判断，否则斜着看的表面会冒出细碎斜纹。<br>- 活动部件要真实运动，比如箱盖绕铰链翻开、奖章绕竖轴旋转。<br>- 待机时主体缓慢左右转动，让人看到侧面和厚度，同时轻微上下浮动；脚下要有随高度变化的柔和投影。<br><br>【如果画面里有 2D... <i>(use CLI for full prompt)</i></small><br><br><code>python3 scripts/opus_videos.py prompt op7418-814408</code></details> |
| <a href="https://x.com/op7418/status/2104085484347818226"><img src="https://media.skillry.dev/opus-5-5/op7418-818226/remake.4373467104.webp" width="200" alt="op7418-818226" loading="lazy" /></a> | <b>ID</b>: #146<br><b>Slug</b>: <code>op7418-818226</code><br><b>Author</b>: <a href="https://x.com/op7418/status/2104085484347818226">@op7418</a><br><b>Duration</b>: 17.69s (16:9)<br><b>Tags</b>: `canvas` `svg` `gsap` `css` | <details><summary><b>View Prompt</b></summary><br><small>帮我做一个原创的手游「高光时刻」动效：[主体，例如：开宝箱 / 段位晋升 / 成就解锁 / Boss 掉落 / 角色升级]。<br>做成一个可以直接打开的单文件网页（HTML + CSS + JS），点一下就能完整播放，效果对标商业手游的结算 / 奖励演出。<br><br>【世界观与风格】<br>- 原创美术，不要模仿任何现有游戏的 IP、角色或 Logo。<br>- 卡通手游风：高饱和配色，统一的深色描边（例如 #1A1033），圆润厚重的造型。<br>- 配色随等级升级：[等级阶梯，例如：普通绿 → 稀有蓝 → 史诗紫 → 传说金]。每升一级都要切换主色、光效颜色和背景色调。<br>- 标题用圆胖的游戏字体（如 Titan One / Lilita One），描黑边，加投影；文字逐字「砸」进画面，带回弹。<br><br>【主体物：做成真 3D】<br>- 用 Three.js 建模，不要用平面 SVG 贴图。要有厚度、倒角，以及符合主体的结构细节（木板缝、铆钉、包边、宝石切面、锁扣等）；木板缝这类细节用真实几何体拼出来，不要贴黑线条。<br>- 着色用卡通分阶（MeshToonMaterial 加 3～4 阶的渐变贴图），配三种灯光：主光、天光、等级色的轮廓光。<br>- 描边用后期边缘检测：渲染法线图 + 深度图，再用着色器画线。外轮廓粗、零件交界细，粗细全程一致；另外做 1.5 倍超采样和多重采样抗锯齿。<br>  不要用「放大一圈的黑色背面」那种描边，那样粗细不均、转角会断。<br>  深度判断的阈值要放宽，接缝主要靠法线判断，否则斜着看的表面会冒出细碎斜纹。<br>- 活动部件要真实运动，比如箱盖绕铰链翻开、奖章绕竖轴旋转。<br>- 待机时主体缓慢左右转动，让人看到侧面和厚度，同时轻微上下浮动；脚下要有随高度变化的柔和投影。<br><br>【如果画面里有 2D... <i>(use CLI for full prompt)</i></small><br><br><code>python3 scripts/opus_videos.py prompt op7418-818226</code></details> |

### Particle Systems, Verlet Physics & Collisions (14 styles)
<p>Closed-form spring dynamics, gravity wells, elastic cloth simulation, particle swarms, and rigid-body ball collisions.</p>

## Render Pipelines & Determinism

### Pipeline 1: Zero-Dependency Canvas + Playwright (Recommended)
```bash
cd templates/canvas
npm install
node render.js
```

### Pipeline 2: HyperFrames Declarative Video
```bash
cd templates/hyperframes
npx hyperframes preview
npx hyperframes render --output master.mp4
```

### Pipeline 3: Remotion React Video
```bash
npx remotion render src/index.ts MainComposition out/video.mp4 --codec=h264 --crf=18
```

## Contributing & Multi-Agent Protocol
When modifying this repository in automated multi-agent environments:
1. Keep `SKILL.md`, `references/catalog.json`, and `README.md` strictly synchronized.
2. Maintain deterministic seek-safe rendering rules.
3. Run `graphify .` and `memoria refresh` upon committing structural changes.
